    [   MemoryDep(name='embedding', index=c0, size=(131072,)),
        MemoryDep(name='gt', index=c0, size=(131072,)),
        MemoryDep(name='primals_1', index=c1, size=(256, 512)),
        MemoryDep(name='reciprocal', index=c0, size=(256, 512))]
buf0.group.device = cuda:0
buf0.group.iteration = (131072, 1)
buf0.sizes = ([256, 512], [])
class buf0_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {z0: 256, z1: 512}
    index0 = z1
    index1 = 512*z0 + z1
    index2 = z0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_1', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('gt', get_index_1)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(load_1, torch.float32)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('embedding', get_index_2)
        mul = ops.mul(to_dtype, load_2)
        constant = ops.constant(1.1111111111111112, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, constant)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_3 = ops.load('reciprocal', get_index_3)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(mul_1, load_3)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(load, mul_2)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        store = ops.store('buf0', get_index_4, mul_3, None)
        return store
